Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the math_logic framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 103. The resulting operator achieves bound O(366/n^4).

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on math_logic structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 103. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 366. QED.

Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(4/n). QED.
